# Learning-400 + Adversarial-400

Status: **planned — 0/400 learning signed, 0/400 adversarial signed**

This is an **800-level paired learning authority**: 400 levels for acquiring knowledge, building understanding, learning from projects, creating native model weights and improving candidate models; and 400 one-for-one adversarial levels that continuously challenge those learning mechanisms.

It remains a depth overlay. It does **not** create `VOL-421+`.

## Core architecture

The learning loop is:

```text
discover/acquire -> normalize/provenance -> read/watch/understand
-> extract/reconcile -> memory/knowledge -> reason/experiment
-> learn skills/project lessons -> curate data -> train candidate weights
-> evaluate -> adversarial challenge -> champion/challenger
-> canary/promote or reject -> observe outcomes -> learn again
```

External web/video/document content is always treated as **untrusted evidence**, never higher-priority instruction authority.

### Web acquisition boundary

The design supports search, sitemaps, public links, feeds/APIs, rendered dynamic public pages and authorized sources. It explicitly does **not** treat authentication bypass, paywall bypass or anti-bot evasion as a learning capability. Every acquired object carries URL/source identity, timestamps, transformation lineage, rights/privacy metadata and freshness state.

### Video/audio learning

Video learning is temporal and multimodal: speech/transcript, keyframes, scene transitions, on-screen text, diagrams, actions, demonstrations and audio events are fused into timestamped evidence while retaining modality-specific provenance and disagreement.

### Native weights and project learning

Skeleton may initialize and train its own candidate weights, perform native pretraining, continual learning, distillation, project-specific adapters, weight merging/editing and architecture search. Project outcomes can feed learning, but they remain project/tenant scoped until deliberately promoted. **Production weights never silently mutate in place.** Improvement produces versioned candidates with immutable lineage, held-out evaluation, paired adversarial qualification, canary and rollback.

## Learning strata — 40 × 10

### L400-S01 — Epistemic Learning Foundations

Scope: observation, claim, hypothesis, evidence, knowledge, uncertainty and wisdom.

- **L400-001 — Epistemic Learning Foundations — Boundary & Objective.** For observation, claim, hypothesis, evidence, knowledge, uncertainty and wisdom, define authority, scope, inputs, outputs, forbidden shortcuts, measurable learning objective and stop conditions. Preserve source/project/tenant isolation, explicit uncertainty, reproducible evidence identities and candidate-only promotion semantics. **Proof:** Exact-head evidence must demonstrate measurable knowledge or learning gain for observation, claim, hypothesis, evidence, knowledge, uncertainty and wisdom, preserve provenance and rollback, pass held-out tests, and satisfy paired adversarial layer A400-001 before this learning layer can be signed complete.
- **L400-002 — Epistemic Learning Foundations — Typed Representation.** For observation, claim, hypothesis, evidence, knowledge, uncertainty and wisdom, define canonical schemas, identities, state transitions, provenance, compatibility and uncertainty semantics. Preserve source/project/tenant isolation, explicit uncertainty, reproducible evidence identities and candidate-only promotion semantics. **Proof:** Exact-head evidence must demonstrate measurable knowledge or learning gain for observation, claim, hypothesis, evidence, knowledge, uncertainty and wisdom, preserve provenance and rollback, pass held-out tests, and satisfy paired adversarial layer A400-002 before this learning layer can be signed complete.
- **L400-003 — Epistemic Learning Foundations — Core Learning Mechanism.** For observation, claim, hypothesis, evidence, knowledge, uncertainty and wisdom, implement the bounded learning mechanism with budgets, cancellation, provenance and explicit candidate outputs. Preserve source/project/tenant isolation, explicit uncertainty, reproducible evidence identities and candidate-only promotion semantics. **Proof:** Exact-head evidence must demonstrate measurable knowledge or learning gain for observation, claim, hypothesis, evidence, knowledge, uncertainty and wisdom, preserve provenance and rollback, pass held-out tests, and satisfy paired adversarial layer A400-003 before this learning layer can be signed complete.
- **L400-004 — Epistemic Learning Foundations — State & Lineage.** For observation, claim, hypothesis, evidence, knowledge, uncertainty and wisdom, persist reproducible inputs, transformations, checkpoints, outputs, causal lineage and deletion/retention bindings. Preserve source/project/tenant isolation, explicit uncertainty, reproducible evidence identities and candidate-only promotion semantics. **Proof:** Exact-head evidence must demonstrate measurable knowledge or learning gain for observation, claim, hypothesis, evidence, knowledge, uncertainty and wisdom, preserve provenance and rollback, pass held-out tests, and satisfy paired adversarial layer A400-004 before this learning layer can be signed complete.
- **L400-005 — Epistemic Learning Foundations — Quality & Calibration.** For observation, claim, hypothesis, evidence, knowledge, uncertainty and wisdom, measure correctness, uncertainty, usefulness, bias, transfer and domain-specific quality against explicit baselines. Preserve source/project/tenant isolation, explicit uncertainty, reproducible evidence identities and candidate-only promotion semantics. **Proof:** Exact-head evidence must demonstrate measurable knowledge or learning gain for observation, claim, hypothesis, evidence, knowledge, uncertainty and wisdom, preserve provenance and rollback, pass held-out tests, and satisfy paired adversarial layer A400-005 before this learning layer can be signed complete.
- **L400-006 — Epistemic Learning Foundations — Scale & Efficiency.** For observation, claim, hypothesis, evidence, knowledge, uncertainty and wisdom, prove resource, latency, cost, throughput, data-efficiency and hardware behavior under realistic scale. Preserve source/project/tenant isolation, explicit uncertainty, reproducible evidence identities and candidate-only promotion semantics. **Proof:** Exact-head evidence must demonstrate measurable knowledge or learning gain for observation, claim, hypothesis, evidence, knowledge, uncertainty and wisdom, preserve provenance and rollback, pass held-out tests, and satisfy paired adversarial layer A400-006 before this learning layer can be signed complete.
- **L400-007 — Epistemic Learning Foundations — Feedback & Adaptation.** For observation, claim, hypothesis, evidence, knowledge, uncertainty and wisdom, consume approved outcomes and corrections while preventing silent authority expansion or in-place production mutation. Preserve source/project/tenant isolation, explicit uncertainty, reproducible evidence identities and candidate-only promotion semantics. **Proof:** Exact-head evidence must demonstrate measurable knowledge or learning gain for observation, claim, hypothesis, evidence, knowledge, uncertainty and wisdom, preserve provenance and rollback, pass held-out tests, and satisfy paired adversarial layer A400-007 before this learning layer can be signed complete.
- **L400-008 — Epistemic Learning Foundations — Robustness & Recovery.** For observation, claim, hypothesis, evidence, knowledge, uncertainty and wisdom, handle drift, interruption, corruption, partial failure, rollback and replay with fail-closed semantics. Preserve source/project/tenant isolation, explicit uncertainty, reproducible evidence identities and candidate-only promotion semantics. **Proof:** Exact-head evidence must demonstrate measurable knowledge or learning gain for observation, claim, hypothesis, evidence, knowledge, uncertainty and wisdom, preserve provenance and rollback, pass held-out tests, and satisfy paired adversarial layer A400-008 before this learning layer can be signed complete.
- **L400-009 — Epistemic Learning Foundations — Independent Evaluation.** For observation, claim, hypothesis, evidence, knowledge, uncertainty and wisdom, run held-out independent evaluation with leakage, contamination, distribution-shift and reproducibility controls. Preserve source/project/tenant isolation, explicit uncertainty, reproducible evidence identities and candidate-only promotion semantics. **Proof:** Exact-head evidence must demonstrate measurable knowledge or learning gain for observation, claim, hypothesis, evidence, knowledge, uncertainty and wisdom, preserve provenance and rollback, pass held-out tests, and satisfy paired adversarial layer A400-009 before this learning layer can be signed complete.
- **L400-010 — Epistemic Learning Foundations — Integration & Promotion Gate.** For observation, claim, hypothesis, evidence, knowledge, uncertainty and wisdom, qualify only when contracts interoperate, paired adversarial evidence is current, rollback exists and independent signatures are present. Preserve source/project/tenant isolation, explicit uncertainty, reproducible evidence identities and candidate-only promotion semantics. **Proof:** Exact-head evidence must demonstrate measurable knowledge or learning gain for observation, claim, hypothesis, evidence, knowledge, uncertainty and wisdom, preserve provenance and rollback, pass held-out tests, and satisfy paired adversarial layer A400-010 before this learning layer can be signed complete.

## Adversarial-learning strata — 40 × 10

The adversarial plane is not a final red-team pass. It is a peer learning system. Every `A400-nnn` is paired to `L400-nnn`, produces durable challenge evidence, and can invalidate downstream qualification when evidence becomes stale.

### A400-S01 — Epistemic Deception & False-Knowledge Attacks

Threat focus: fabricated claims, confident nonsense, contradiction floods, ambiguous truth and uncertainty manipulation.

- **A400-001 — Epistemic Deception & False-Knowledge Attacks — Threat Model & Invariants.** Against paired learning layer L400-001, define attacker capabilities, protected invariants, forbidden attacker shortcuts and measurable failure criteria. Focus on fabricated claims, confident nonsense, contradiction floods, ambiguous truth and uncertainty manipulation. Attacks execute only in bounded test/sandbox contexts and cannot grant authority or promote models. **Proof:** Exact-head red-team evidence must reproduce relevant attacks for fabricated claims, confident nonsense, contradiction floods, ambiguous truth and uncertainty manipulation, show detection/containment/recovery or explicit residual risk, and bind findings to paired learning layer L400-001.
- **A400-002 — Epistemic Deception & False-Knowledge Attacks — Attack Corpus & Fixture Construction.** Against paired learning layer L400-002, build reproducible benign/adversarial fixtures with provenance, labels, seeds and expected outcomes. Focus on fabricated claims, confident nonsense, contradiction floods, ambiguous truth and uncertainty manipulation. Attacks execute only in bounded test/sandbox contexts and cannot grant authority or promote models. **Proof:** Exact-head red-team evidence must reproduce relevant attacks for fabricated claims, confident nonsense, contradiction floods, ambiguous truth and uncertainty manipulation, show detection/containment/recovery or explicit residual risk, and bind findings to paired learning layer L400-002.

## Finality

`L400-400` and `A400-400` are paired finality gates. The learning system is not complete until **400/400 learning + 400/400 adversarial** layers are simultaneously current, independently verified and signed. Initial completion is intentionally **0/800**.
